<div style="break-before: page; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 12pt"><p style="text-align: center; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt"></p></div><!-- Field: /Page --><p style="text-align: center; font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: center"><span style="font-weight: bold;"><span id="a_007" style="display: inline-block;"></span>CROWN RESERVE ACQUISITION CORP. I</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: center"><span style="font-weight: bold;">NOTES TO CONDENSED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: center"><span style="font-weight: bold;">MARCH 31, 2026</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: center">(Unaudited)</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: center">&#160;</p>
 <ix:nonNumeric name="us-gaap:NatureOfOperations" contextRef="cref_111832914" escape="true" id="ixv-1131"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Note 1 &#8212; Description of Organization and Business Operations</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> Crown Reserve Acquisition Corp. I (the &#8220;Company&#8221;) was incorporated as a Cayman Islands exempted company on <ix:nonNumeric name="dei:EntityIncorporationDateOfIncorporation" contextRef="cref_111832914" format="ixt:date-monthname-day-year-en" id="ixv-3416">April 29, 2025</ix:nonNumeric>. The Company was formed for the purpose of effecting a merger, capital stock exchange, asset acquisition, stock purchase, reorganization or similar business combination with <ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:NumberOfBusinessesAcquired" contextRef="cref_111832914" unitRef="uref_572717868" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt-sec:numwordsen" id="ixv-3417">one</ix:nonFraction> or more businesses (the &#8220;Business Combination&#8221;). </p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> All activity for the period from <ix:nonNumeric name="crac:YearOfInception" contextRef="cref_111832914" id="ixv-3418">April 29, 2025 (inception) through March 31, 2026</ix:nonNumeric> relates to the Company&#8217;s formation, the Initial Public Offering (the &#8220;IPO&#8221;), and identifying a target company for the Business Combination, including the entry into the Business Combination Agreement described below. The Company will not generate any operating revenues until after the completion of its initial Business Combination, at the earliest. The Company generates non-operating income in the form of dividends earned on marketable securities held in the Trust Account (as defined below). </p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> The Company&#8217;s sponsor is Crown Acquisition Sponsor LLC, a Delaware limited liability company (the &#8220;Sponsor&#8221;). The registration statement for the Company&#8217;s IPO was declared effective on September 26, 2025. On November 10, 2025, the Company consummated its IPO of <ix:nonFraction name="crac:UnitsIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues" contextRef="cref_564448803" unitRef="uref_1275643730" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3419">17,250,000</ix:nonFraction> units (the &#8220;Units&#8221;), including the purchase by the underwriters of <ix:nonFraction name="crac:StockIssuedDuringPeriodOptionsToPurchaseUnits" contextRef="cref_886094707" unitRef="uref_1275643730" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3420">2,250,000</ix:nonFraction> additional Units at the offering price, reflecting the full exercise of the over-allotment option. The Units were sold at a price of $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SharesIssuedPricePerShare" contextRef="cref_1825908935" unitRef="uref_2008090408" scale="0" decimals="2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3421">10.00</ix:nonFraction> per Unit, generating gross proceeds to the Company of $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ProceedsFromIssuanceInitialPublicOffering" contextRef="cref_1265068831" unitRef="uref_1130685022" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3422">172,500,000</ix:nonFraction>. Each Unit consisted of one Class A ordinary share, one-half of one redeemable warrant, and one right (a &#8220;Public Right&#8221;) to receive one-fifth (1/5) of one Class A ordinary share upon the consummation of an initial Business Combination. </p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> Simultaneously with the closing of the IPO, the Company consummated the private sale of an aggregate of <ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues" contextRef="cref_15338407" unitRef="uref_1275643730" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3423">375,000</ix:nonFraction> private placement units (the &#8220;Private Placement Units&#8221;), of which <ix:nonFraction name="crac:StockIssuedDuringPeriodOptionsToPurchaseUnits" contextRef="cref_705911262" unitRef="uref_1275643730" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3424">175,000</ix:nonFraction> were purchased by the Sponsor and <ix:nonFraction name="crac:StockIssuedDuringPeriodOptionsToPurchaseUnits" contextRef="cref_888143275" unitRef="uref_1275643730" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3425">200,000</ix:nonFraction> were purchased by Polaris Advisory Partners (&#8220;Polaris&#8221;), each at a price of $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SharesIssuedPricePerShare" contextRef="cref_16156336" unitRef="uref_2008090408" scale="0" decimals="2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3426">8.00</ix:nonFraction> per Private Placement Unit, generating total proceeds of $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ProceedsFromIssuanceOfPrivatePlacement" contextRef="cref_15338407" unitRef="uref_1130685022" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3427">3,000,000</ix:nonFraction>. </p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> Following the closing of the IPO, an amount of $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ProceedsFromIssuanceOfPrivatePlacement" contextRef="cref_1996041497" unitRef="uref_1130685022" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3428">172,500,000</ix:nonFraction> ($<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SaleOfStockPricePerShare" contextRef="cref_175746476" unitRef="uref_2008090408" scale="0" decimals="2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3429">10.00</ix:nonFraction> per Unit) from the net proceeds of the sale of the Units and the Private Placement Units was placed in a trust account (the &#8220;Trust Account&#8221;) located in the United States, maintained by Equiniti Trust Company, LLC as trustee, and invested only in U.S. government treasury obligations with a maturity of 185 days or less or in money market funds meeting certain conditions under Rule 2a-7 under the Investment Company Act of 1940, as amended, which invest only in direct U.S. government treasury obligations. As of March 31, 2026, the Trust Account held $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AssetsHeldInTrustNoncurrent" contextRef="cref_175746476" unitRef="uref_1130685022" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3430">174,928,579</ix:nonFraction>, including $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:MarketableSecurities" contextRef="cref_175746476" unitRef="uref_1130685022" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3431">2,428,579</ix:nonFraction> of dividends earned since the IPO closing date (of which $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:Dividends" contextRef="cref_111832914" unitRef="uref_1130685022" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3432">1,524,741</ix:nonFraction> was earned during the three months ended March 31, 2026). </p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> The Company&#8217;s Business Combination must be with one or more target businesses that together have a fair market value equal to at least <ix:nonFraction name="crac:FairMarketValuePercentage" contextRef="cref_873205602" unitRef="uref_231006451" scale="-2" decimals="2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3433">80</ix:nonFraction>% of the net balance in the Trust Account (excluding any deferred underwriting commissions and taxes payable on income earned on the Trust Account) at the time of the signing of an agreement to enter into a Business Combination. </p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;"><i>&#160;</i></span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;"><i>Business Combination Agreement</i></span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">On March 30, 2026, the Company, CRAC Merger Sub Inc., a Delaware corporation and wholly owned direct subsidiary of the Company (&#8220;Merger Sub&#8221;), and Carvix, Inc., a Delaware corporation (&#8220;Carvix&#8221;), entered into a Business Combination Agreement (the &#8220;Business Combination Agreement&#8221;). Subject to the terms and conditions of the Business Combination Agreement, (i) the Company will effect a domestication to Delaware (the &#8220;Domestication&#8221;), (ii) immediately thereafter, Merger Sub will merge with and into Carvix (the &#8220;Merger&#8221;), with Carvix surviving as a wholly owned subsidiary of the Company (as domesticated), and (iii) the parties intend that the Domestication qualify as a &#8220;reorganization&#8221; within the meaning of Section 368(a)(1)(F) of the Internal Revenue Code of 1986, as amended (the &#8220;Code&#8221;), and that the Merger qualify as a &#8220;reorganization&#8221; within the meaning of Section 368(a) of the Code.</p><ix:exclude><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"></p><!-- Field: Page; Sequence: 7; Value: 1 --><div style="margin-top: 12pt; margin-bottom: 6pt; border-bottom: Black 1.5pt solid"><p style="font: normal 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: center; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt"><!-- Field: Sequence; Type: Arabic; Name: PageNo -->5<!-- Field: /Sequence --></p></div><div style="break-before: page; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 12pt"><p style="text-align: center; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt"></p></div><!-- Field: /Page --><p style="text-align: center; font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: center"><span style="font-weight: bold;">CROWN RESERVE ACQUISITION CORP. Pursuant to a SPAC Founders Stock Letter executed concurrently with the Business Combination Agreement, the Sponsor irrevocably waived any anti-dilution or conversion ratio adjustment rights that would otherwise be triggered by the private placements, the Merger or other transactions contemplated by the Business Combination Agreement.</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The post-closing board of directors will consist of five members: four nominated by Carvix (including one independent director approved by the Sponsor) and one nominated by the Sponsor (who will be an independent director approved by Carvix). Carvix&#8217;s existing management team will continue to lead the combined company following the Closing.</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">Consummation of the transactions is subject to customary conditions, including, among others, (i) the requisite approval by the Company&#8217;s stockholders of the proposals described in the Business Combination Agreement, (ii) delivery of the Carvix stockholder written consent sufficient to constitute the requisite Carvix stockholder approval, (iii) the Domestication, (iv) the U.S. Securities and Exchange Commission (the &#8220;SEC&#8221;) declaring effective the Registration Statement on Form S-4 to be filed by the Company, (v) approval for listing on Nasdaq of the shares of the Company&#8217;s common stock to be issued in the transactions (including the earnout shares), (vi) expiration or termination of any applicable waiting period under the Hart-Scott-Rodino Antitrust Improvements Act, if required, (vii) satisfaction of the Company&#8217;s net tangible asset or &#8220;penny stock&#8221; condition, and (viii) the Company&#8217;s having at the Closing at least the &#8220;Minimum Cash Amount,&#8221; after giving effect to redemptions, payment of transaction expenses and repayment of indebtedness.</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Business Combination Agreement may be terminated under certain customary and limited circumstances at any time prior to the Effective Time, including by either the Company or Carvix if the Effective Time has not occurred on or prior to September 30, 2026 (the &#8220;Outside Date&#8221;), unless the breach of the terminating party was the principal cause of the failure of the Closing to occur by such date.</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">Concurrently with the execution of the Business Combination Agreement, the Sponsor and Carvix entered into a SPAC Founders Stock Letter, and the Company, Carvix and certain key Carvix stockholders entered into a Stockholder Support Agreement. At the Closing, the Company (as domesticated), the Sponsor, Carvix founders and certain other holders will enter into an Investor Rights Agreement covering governance matters (including initial board composition) and registration rights.</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The foregoing description of the Business Combination Agreement and the related agreements does not purport to be complete and is qualified in its entirety by reference to the full text thereof, copies of which were filed as exhibits to the Company&#8217;s Current Report on Form 8-K filed with the SEC on April 3, 2026. The Business Combination had not closed as of March 31, 2026, and accordingly, no Carvix assets, liabilities, results of operations, merger consideration, or earnout shares are reflected in the unaudited condensed financial statements.</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> </p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;"><i>Going Concern</i></span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> As of March 31, 2026, the Company held no cash and had a working capital deficit funded entirely by the Sponsor and is dependent on Sponsor funding and/or additional financing to fund operations and transaction costs. The Company has until November 10, 2026 (automatically extended to February 10, 2027 upon the execution of the Business Combination Agreement on March 30, 2026) to consummate a Business Combination. If the Company does not complete a Business Combination within the Combination Period, the Company will cease all operations except for winding up and will redeem <ix:nonFraction name="crac:ConversionOfAggregatePercentage" contextRef="cref_473889171" unitRef="uref_231006451" scale="-2" decimals="2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3446">100</ix:nonFraction>% of the outstanding Class A ordinary shares. In connection with the Company&#8217;s assessment of going concern considerations in accordance with ASC 205-40, management has determined that the mandatory liquidation, should a Business Combination not occur, and the potential subsequent dissolution raise substantial doubt about the Company&#8217;s ability to continue as a going concern for one year from the date these condensed financial statements are issued. No adjustments have been made to the carrying amounts of assets or liabilities should the Company be required to liquidate after the Combination Period. </p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> </p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> In order to fund working capital deficiencies or finance transaction costs in connection with a Business Combination, the Sponsor, members of the Company&#8217;s management team or any of their affiliates may, but are not obligated to, loan the Company funds as may be required (&#8220;Working Capital Loans&#8221;). If the Company completes a Business Combination, the Company would repay such Working Capital Loans at that time. In the event that a Business Combination does not close, the Company may use a portion of the proceeds held outside the Trust Account to repay such Working Capital Loans, but no proceeds held in the Trust Account would be used to repay such Working Capital Loans. Up to $<ix:nonFraction name="crac:WorkingCapitalLoans" contextRef="cref_111832914" unitRef="uref_1130685022" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3447">5,000,000</ix:nonFraction> of such Working Capital Loans may be convertible into Private Placement Units at a price of $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SharesIssuedPricePerShare" contextRef="cref_473889171" unitRef="uref_2008090408" scale="0" decimals="2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3448">8.00</ix:nonFraction> per unit, at the option of the lender. As of March 31, 2026, there were no amounts outstanding under any Working Capital Loans. </p></ix:nonNumeric> <p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&#160;</p><!-- Field: Page; Sequence: 8; Value: 1 --><div style="margin-top: 12pt; margin-bottom: 6pt; border-bottom: Black 1.5pt solid"><p style="font: normal 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: center; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt"><!-- Field: Sequence; Type: Arabic; Name: PageNo -->6<!-- Field: /Sequence --></p></div><div style="break-before: page; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 12pt"><p style="text-align: center; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt"></p></div><!-- Field: /Page --><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: center"><span style="font-weight: bold;">CROWN RESERVE ACQUISITION CORP.  <ix:nonNumeric name="us-gaap:SignificantAccountingPoliciesTextBlock" contextRef="cref_111832914" escape="true" id="ixv-1207"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Note 2 &#8212; Summary of Significant Accounting Policies</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&#160;</p><ix:nonNumeric name="us-gaap:BasisOfAccountingPolicyPolicyTextBlock" contextRef="cref_111832914" escape="true" id="ixv-1211"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;"><i>Basis of Presentation</i></span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The accompanying unaudited condensed financial statements have been prepared in accordance with accounting principles generally accepted in the United States of America (&#8220;U.S. GAAP&#8221;) for interim financial information and pursuant to the rules and regulations of the SEC. Accordingly, they do not include all of the information and footnotes required by U.S. GAAP for complete financial statements. In the opinion of management, all adjustments (consisting of normal recurring adjustments) considered necessary for a fair presentation have been included. Operating results for the three months ended March 31, 2026 are not necessarily indicative of the results that may be expected for the year ending December 31, 2026, or any future period. The accompanying unaudited condensed financial statements should be read in conjunction with the audited financial statements and notes thereto for the period from April 29, 2025 (inception) through December 31, 2025 included in the Company&#8217;s Annual Report on Form 10-K filed with the SEC on March 27, 2026.</p></ix:nonNumeric><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><ix:nonNumeric name="crac:EmergingGrowthCompanyPolicyTextBlock" contextRef="cref_111832914" escape="true" id="ixv-1218"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;"><i>Emerging Growth Company</i></span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Company is an &#8220;emerging growth company,&#8221; as defined in Section 2(a) of the Securities Act of 1933, as amended (the &#8220;Securities Act&#8221;), as modified by the Jumpstart Our Business Startups Act of 2012 (the &#8220;JOBS Act&#8221;), and it may take advantage of certain exemptions from various reporting requirements that are applicable to other public companies that are not emerging growth companies, including, but not limited to, not being required to comply with the auditor attestation requirements of Section 404 of the Sarbanes-Oxley Act, reduced disclosure obligations regarding executive compensation in its periodic reports and proxy statements, and exemptions from the requirements of holding a nonbinding advisory vote on executive compensation and shareholder approval of any golden parachute payments not previously approved.</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">Further, Section 102(b)(1) of the JOBS Act exempts emerging growth companies from being required to comply with new or revised financial accounting standards until private companies (that is, those that have not had a Securities Act registration statement declared effective or do not have a class of securities registered under the Securities Exchange Act of 1934, as amended (the &#8220;Exchange Act&#8221;)) are required to comply with the new or revised financial accounting standards. The JOBS Act provides that an emerging growth company can elect to opt out of the extended transition period and comply with the requirements that apply to non-emerging growth companies, but any such election to opt out is irrevocable. The Company has elected not to opt out of such extended transition period, which means that when a standard is issued or revised and it has different application dates for public or private companies, the Company, as an emerging growth company, can adopt the new or revised standard at the time private companies adopt the new or revised standard. This may make comparison of the Company&#8217;s financial statements with another public company that is neither an emerging growth company nor an emerging growth company that has opted out of using the extended transition period difficult or impossible because of the potential differences in accounting standards used.</p></ix:nonNumeric><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><ix:nonNumeric name="us-gaap:UseOfEstimates" contextRef="cref_111832914" escape="true" id="ixv-1227"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;"><i>Use of Estimates</i></span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The preparation of financial statements in conformity with U.S. GAAP requires the Company&#8217;s management to make estimates and assumptions that affect the reported amounts of assets and liabilities and disclosure of contingent assets and liabilities at the date of the financial statements and the reported amounts of revenues and expenses during the reporting period. Actual results could differ from those estimates.</p></ix:nonNumeric><ix:exclude><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"></p><!-- Field: Page; Sequence: 9; Value: 1 --><div style="margin-top: 12pt; margin-bottom: 6pt; border-bottom: Black 1.5pt solid"><p style="font: normal 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: center; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt"><!-- Field: Sequence; Type: Arabic; Name: PageNo -->7<!-- Field: /Sequence --></p></div><div style="break-before: page; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 12pt"><p style="text-align: center; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt"></p></div><!-- Field: /Page --><p style="text-align: center; font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: center"><span style="font-weight: bold;">CROWN RESERVE ACQUISITION CORP. The Company had no cash or cash equivalents held outside the Trust Account as of March 31, 2026 and December 31, 2025.</p></ix:nonNumeric><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><ix:nonNumeric name="us-gaap:ConcentrationRiskCreditRisk" contextRef="cref_111832914" escape="true" id="ixv-1256"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;"><i>Concentration of Credit Risk</i></span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> Financial instruments that potentially subject the Company to concentrations of credit risk consist of cash accounts in a financial institution. During the three months ended March 31, 2026, all funding for the Company&#8217;s operations was provided by the Sponsor through the Sponsor&#8217;s account on the Company&#8217;s behalf, which at times may exceed the Federal Deposit Insurance Corporation (&#8220;FDIC&#8221;) coverage limit of $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:CashFDICInsuredAmount" contextRef="cref_473889171" unitRef="uref_1130685022" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3449">250,000</ix:nonFraction>. As of March 31, 2026, the Company has not experienced losses on these accounts and management believes the Company is not exposed to significant risks on such accounts. </p></ix:nonNumeric><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><ix:nonNumeric name="us-gaap:MarketableSecuritiesPolicy" contextRef="cref_111832914" escape="true" id="ixv-1263"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;"><i>Marketable Securities Held in Trust Account</i></span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> As of March 31, 2026 and December 31, 2025, substantially all of the assets held in the Trust Account were held in money market funds invested primarily in U.S. government securities with a maturity of <ix:nonNumeric name="crac:NoOfMaturityDays" contextRef="cref_111832914" format="ixt-sec:durday" id="ixv-3450">185</ix:nonNumeric> days or less. All of the Company&#8217;s investments held in the Trust Account are classified as trading securities. Trading securities are presented on the balance sheet at fair value at the end of each reporting period. Gains and losses resulting from the change in fair value of investments held in the Trust Account are included in dividends earned on marketable securities held in Trust Account in the accompanying statement of operations. The estimated fair values of investments held in the Trust Account are determined using available market information and are classified as Level 1 inputs utilizing quoted prices (unadjusted) in active markets for identical assets. As of March 31, 2026 and December 31, 2025, the Trust Account held $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AssetsHeldInTrustNoncurrent" contextRef="cref_473889171" unitRef="uref_1130685022" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3451">174,928,579</ix:nonFraction> and $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AssetsHeldInTrustNoncurrent" contextRef="cref_1717737502" unitRef="uref_1130685022" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3452">173,403,838</ix:nonFraction>, respectively. Through March 31, 2026, the Company has <span style="-sec-ix-hidden:fc_2123653117">not</span> withdrawn any interest income from the Trust Account. </p></ix:nonNumeric><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><ix:nonNumeric name="us-gaap:FairValueOfFinancialInstrumentsPolicy" contextRef="cref_111832914" escape="true" id="ixv-1271"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;"><i>Fair Value of Financial Instruments</i></span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The fair value of the Company&#8217;s assets and liabilities, which qualify as financial instruments under FASB ASC 820, &#8220;Fair Value Measurement,&#8221; approximates the carrying amounts represented in the balance sheet, primarily due to their short-term nature, except for the warrant liability, which is measured at fair value on a recurring basis (see Note 5).</p></ix:nonNumeric><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> The Company complies with the requirements of ASC 340-10-S99-1, SEC Staff Accounting Bulletin Topic 5A &#8212; &#8220;Expenses of Offering.&#8221; Offering costs of $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DeferredOfferingCosts" contextRef="cref_473889171" unitRef="uref_1130685022" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3453">2,552,133</ix:nonFraction> (consisting of $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:OtherUnderwritingExpense" id="fc_2090487478" contextRef="cref_111832914" unitRef="uref_1130685022" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal">1,725,000</ix:nonFraction> in upfront underwriting commissions and $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:OtherDeferredCostsNet" id="fc_1347242449" contextRef="cref_175746476" unitRef="uref_1130685022" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal">827,133</ix:nonFraction> in other offering costs) were charged to Class A temporary equity upon the completion of the IPO. The deferred underwriting commission of $<ix:nonFraction name="crac:DeferredUnderwritingCommissionPayable" contextRef="cref_1996041497" unitRef="uref_1130685022" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3456">300,000</ix:nonFraction>, payable only upon consummation of a Business Combination, is recorded as a liability and excluded from offering costs charged to equity. </p></ix:nonNumeric><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><ix:nonNumeric name="crac:WarrantLiabilitiesPolicyTextBlock" contextRef="cref_111832914" escape="true" id="ixv-1285"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;"><i>Warrant Liabilities</i></span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> The Company accounts for the redeemable warrants issued in the IPO (the &#8220;Public Warrants&#8221;) as liabilities under ASC 815-40, recognized at fair value upon issuance with subsequent changes recognized in the statement of operations per ASC 815-40-35-4. The Public Warrants are classified as liabilities because the Warrant Agreement contains a provision under which warrant holders may receive cash in connection with a tender offer if such tender offer is accepted by holders of more than <ix:nonFraction name="crac:PercentageOfReceiveCashInConnectionWithTenderOfferAcceptedOrdinaryShareholders" contextRef="cref_111832914" unitRef="uref_231006451" scale="-2" decimals="2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3457">50</ix:nonFraction>% of Class A ordinary shares &#8212; an event outside the sole control of the Company. Fair value is estimated using a 500-step Cox-Ross-Rubinstein binomial lattice model (see Note 5). </p></ix:nonNumeric><ix:exclude><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"></p><!-- Field: Page; Sequence: 10; Value: 1 --><div style="margin-top: 12pt; margin-bottom: 6pt; border-bottom: Black 1.5pt solid"><p style="font: normal 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: center; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt"><!-- Field: Sequence; Type: Arabic; Name: PageNo -->8<!-- Field: /Sequence --></p></div><div style="break-before: page; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 12pt"><p style="text-align: center; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt"></p></div><!-- Field: /Page --><p style="text-align: center; font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: center"><span style="font-weight: bold;">CROWN RESERVE ACQUISITION CORP. I</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: center"><span style="font-weight: bold;">NOTES TO CONDENSED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: center"><span style="font-weight: bold;">MARCH 31, 2026</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: center">(Unaudited)</p></ix:exclude><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><ix:nonNumeric name="crac:PublicRightsPolicyTextBlock" contextRef="cref_111832914" escape="true" id="ixv-1307"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;"><i>Public Rights</i></span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Company accounts for the rights issued in the IPO (the &#8220;Public Rights&#8221;) as permanent equity under ASC 815-40. Each Public Right entitles the holder to receive 0.20 of a Class A ordinary share upon consummation of a Business Combination for no additional consideration. Public Rights are indexed to the Company&#8217;s own stock, contain no net-cash settlement provisions, and expire worthless in liquidation. The fair value of Public Rights at issuance was recorded within additional paid-in capital. Public Rights are not remeasured subsequent to initial recognition.</p></ix:nonNumeric><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><ix:nonNumeric name="us-gaap:SharesSubjectToMandatoryRedemptionChangesInRedemptionValuePolicyTextBlock" contextRef="cref_111832914" escape="true" id="ixv-1314"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;"><i>Class A Ordinary Shares Subject to Possible Redemption</i></span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> All <ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:TemporaryEquitySharesOutstanding" contextRef="cref_61118182" unitRef="uref_1275643730" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3458">17,250,000</ix:nonFraction> Class A ordinary shares sold as part of the Units in the IPO are classified as temporary equity under ASC 480-10-S99 as they are redeemable in connection with a Business Combination vote, tender offer, or failure to complete a Business Combination within the Combination Period &#8212; all events outside the sole control of the Company. The Company accretes the carrying value of Class A ordinary shares to their redemption value at each reporting date, with accretion charged first to additional paid-in capital until exhausted, and the remainder charged to accumulated deficit. As of March 31, 2026 and December 31, 2025, the redemption value of the Class A ordinary shares is $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:TemporaryEquityValueExcludingAdditionalPaidInCapital" contextRef="cref_61118182" unitRef="uref_1130685022" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3459">174,928,579</ix:nonFraction> and $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:TemporaryEquityValueExcludingAdditionalPaidInCapital" contextRef="cref_836287118" unitRef="uref_1130685022" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3460">173,403,838</ix:nonFraction>, respectively. </p></ix:nonNumeric><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><ix:nonNumeric name="us-gaap:EarningsPerSharePolicyTextBlock" contextRef="cref_111832914" escape="true" id="ixv-1321"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;"><i>Net Loss Per Ordinary Share</i></span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Company applies the two-class method under ASC 260 to compute earnings (loss) per ordinary share. Both Class A and Class B ordinary shares are participating securities sharing equally in dividends and distributions. Net income (or net loss) is allocated between classes based on weighted-average shares outstanding. Accretion of Class A ordinary shares to redemption value is a direct equity charge excluded from the EPS numerator. The Company&#8217;s outstanding Public Warrants and Public Rights are excluded from the computation of diluted earnings (loss) per share when their effect would be antidilutive (see Note 10).</p></ix:nonNumeric><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><ix:nonNumeric name="us-gaap:IncomeTaxPolicyTextBlock" contextRef="cref_111832914" escape="true" id="ixv-1328"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;"><i>Income Taxes</i></span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> The Company is a Cayman Islands exempted company not subject to income taxes under current Cayman Islands law. The income tax provision for the period is <ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:IncomeTaxExpenseBenefit" contextRef="cref_111832914" unitRef="uref_1130685022" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:fixed-zero" id="ixv-3461">zero</ix:nonFraction>. Management has determined there are <span style="-sec-ix-hidden:fc_1976321950">no</span> uncertain tax positions that would require recognition or disclosure. </p></ix:nonNumeric><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><ix:nonNumeric name="us-gaap:SegmentReportingPolicyPolicyTextBlock" contextRef="cref_111832914" escape="true" id="ixv-1336"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;"><i>Segment Reporting</i></span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> The Company complies with ASC 280, &#8220;Segment Reporting,&#8221; which establishes standards for reporting information about operating segments. Operating segments are defined as components of an enterprise for which separate financial information is available that is regularly evaluated by the Company&#8217;s chief operating decision maker (&#8220;CODM&#8221;) in deciding how to allocate resources and assess performance. The Company has determined that it operates as a <span style="-sec-ix-hidden:fc_400378910">single</span> operating segment (see Note 11). </p></ix:nonNumeric><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><ix:nonNumeric name="us-gaap:NewAccountingPronouncementsPolicyPolicyTextBlock" contextRef="cref_111832914" escape="true" id="ixv-1344"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;"><i>Recent Accounting Pronouncements</i></span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">Management does not believe that any recently issued, but not yet effective, accounting pronouncements would have a material effect on the Company&#8217;s condensed financial statements.</p></ix:nonNumeric></ix:nonNumeric> <p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&#160;</p><!-- Field: Page; Sequence: 11; Value: 1 --><div style="margin-top: 12pt; margin-bottom: 6pt; border-bottom: Black 1.5pt solid"><p style="font: normal 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: center; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt"><!-- Field: Sequence; Type: Arabic; Name: PageNo -->9<!-- Field: /Sequence --></p></div><div style="break-before: page; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 12pt"><p style="text-align: center; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt"></p></div><!-- Field: /Page --><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: center"><span style="font-weight: bold;">CROWN RESERVE ACQUISITION CORP. Each Private Placement Unit consists of one Class A ordinary share, one-half of one Private Placement Warrant, and one Private Placement Right. The Private Placement Units are identical to the Units sold in the IPO, except that: (i) the Private Placement Warrants will be non-redeemable and exercisable on a cashless basis so long as they are held by the Sponsor or its permitted transferees; and (ii) the Private Placement Rights will automatically convert into Class A ordinary shares upon consummation of a Business Combination. </p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;"><i>Sponsor Funding of Company Operations</i></span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Company holds no operating cash. The Sponsor pays formation and operating costs on the Company&#8217;s behalf, which are recorded as Due from related party (or, when net obligations are owed to the Sponsor, as a Promissory Note &#8212; Related Party) on the balance sheet.</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;"><i>Due from Related Party</i></span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> As of March 31, 2026 and December 31, 2025, the Company had a receivable of $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:OtherReceivablesNetCurrent" contextRef="cref_1471217968" unitRef="uref_1130685022" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3494">210,670</ix:nonFraction> and $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:NoninterestExpenseOfferingCost" contextRef="cref_1119550428" unitRef="uref_1130685022" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3495">448,082</ix:nonFraction>, respectively, due from the Sponsor. This balance represents Private Placement proceeds received by the Sponsor on the Company&#8217;s behalf that were designated for use outside the Trust Account, net of formation and operating costs paid by the Sponsor on the Company&#8217;s behalf and other amounts owed to the Sponsor. The amounts are non-interest bearing, unsecured and due on demand. </p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><ix:nonNumeric name="us-gaap:ScheduleOfRelatedPartyTransactionsTableTextBlock" contextRef="cref_111832914" escape="true" id="ixv-1422"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif;  <ix:nonNumeric name="us-gaap:DerivativeInstrumentsAndHedgingActivitiesDisclosureTextBlock" contextRef="cref_111832914" escape="true" id="ixv-1486"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Note 5 &#8212; Warrant Liabilities</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> The Company issued <ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues" contextRef="cref_1876731423" unitRef="uref_1275643730" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3501">8,625,000</ix:nonFraction> redeemable Public Warrants as part of the IPO Units (each Unit contained one-half of one Public Warrant). Each whole Public Warrant entitles the holder to purchase one Class A ordinary share at an exercise price of $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ClassOfWarrantOrRightExercisePriceOfWarrantsOrRights1" contextRef="cref_891473963" unitRef="uref_2008090408" scale="0" decimals="2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3502">11.50</ix:nonFraction> per share, subject to adjustment. The Public Warrants will become exercisable <ix:nonNumeric name="crac:PeriodOfWarrantsAfterPublicOffering" contextRef="cref_1761659880" format="ixt-sec:durday" id="ixv-3503">30</ix:nonNumeric> days after the completion of a Business Combination and will expire <ix:nonNumeric name="crac:ExpirationOfWarrantsTerm" contextRef="cref_111832914" id="ixv-3504">five years</ix:nonNumeric> after the completion of a Business Combination, or earlier upon redemption or liquidation. </p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> The Company may redeem the outstanding Public Warrants at a price of $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ClassOfWarrantOrRightExercisePriceOfWarrantsOrRights1" contextRef="cref_1579157876" unitRef="uref_2008090408" scale="0" decimals="2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3505">0.01</ix:nonFraction> per warrant if the Class A ordinary share price equals or exceeds $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SharePrice" contextRef="cref_61118182" unitRef="uref_2008090408" scale="0" decimals="2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3506">18.00</ix:nonFraction> per share for any <ix:nonNumeric name="crac:MinimumTradingDaysForMeetingSharePriceCondition" contextRef="cref_784081467" format="ixt-sec:durday" id="ixv-3507">20</ix:nonNumeric> trading days within a <ix:nonNumeric name="crac:MeasurementPeriodForConsideringSharePriceCondition" contextRef="cref_784081467" format="ixt-sec:durday" id="ixv-3508">30</ix:nonNumeric>-trading day period commencing at least <ix:nonNumeric name="crac:MaximumPeriodForFilingAfterBusinessCombination" contextRef="cref_784081467" format="ixt-sec:durday" id="ixv-3509">150</ix:nonNumeric> days after a Business Combination. </p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> The Public Warrants are classified as liabilities at fair value in accordance with ASC 815-40. The Company determined that the Public Warrants do not meet the criteria for equity classification because the Warrant Agreement contains a provision pursuant to which the warrant holders could receive cash in connection with a tender offer accepted by more than <ix:nonFraction name="crac:PercentageOfReceiveCashInConnectionWithTenderOfferAcceptedOrdinaryShareholders" contextRef="cref_105374291" unitRef="uref_231006451" scale="-2" decimals="2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3510">50</ix:nonFraction>% of the Class A ordinary shareholders &#8212; an event that is outside the Company&#8217;s control. Accordingly, the Public Warrants are recorded as liabilities at fair value, with changes in fair value recognized in the statement of operations each reporting period. </p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;"><i>Fair Value Measurement</i></span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> The Public Warrants are classified as Level 3 in the fair value hierarchy as they are valued using a 500-step Cox-Ross-Rubinstein binomial lattice model with unobservable inputs. <ix:nonNumeric name="us-gaap:ScheduleOfDerivativeAssetsAtFairValueTableTextBlock" contextRef="cref_111832914" continuedAt="continuation_493215627" escape="true" id="ixv-3511">The significant inputs used in the valuation as of March 31, 2026 and December 31, 2025 are summarized below:</ix:nonNumeric> </p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: left">&#160;</td> </tr></tbody></table></ix:nonNumeric><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> The loss on the change in fair value of the warrant liability of $(<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:FairValueAdjustmentOfWarrants" contextRef="cref_1927099639" unitRef="uref_1130685022" scale="0" decimals="0" sign="-" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3525">1,604,397</ix:nonFraction>) for the three months ended March 31, 2026 is included in &#8220;Change in fair value of warrant liability&#8221; in the accompanying condensed statement of operations. The expected volatility input was derived as the simple average of the implied volatilities of four peer pre-business-combination SPAC public warrants observable in active markets at the measurement date (SOCAW, ORIQW, BCARW, and CCIIW), rounded to the nearest 10 basis points, applied within the framework documented in the Company&#8217;s warrant valuation memorandum, as supplemented for Q1 2026. Implied volatility for each peer was computed by inverting the Black-Scholes-Merton European call option pricing formula at the peer&#8217;s observed warrant market price, observed share price, the standard $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SharePrice" contextRef="cref_2008090408" unitRef="uref_2008090408" scale="0" decimals="2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3526">11.50</ix:nonFraction> exercise price, the peer&#8217;s remaining contractual term, and the corresponding risk-free rate. While peer warrant market prices are Level 2 observable inputs under ASC 820, the resulting peer-basket average implied volatility applied within the Company&#8217;s framework remains a Level 3 input, consistent with the Level 3 classification of the warrant liability. A 100-basis-point increase or decrease in the volatility input would have increased or decreased the warrant liability by approximately $<ix:nonFraction name="crac:IncreaseDecreaseInWarrantLiability" contextRef="cref_1927099639" unitRef="uref_1130685022" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3527">285,000</ix:nonFraction> at March 31, 2026, with a corresponding increase or decrease, respectively, in net loss for the period. </p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> </p><ix:exclude><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"></p><!-- Field: Page; Sequence: 14; Value: 1 --><div style="margin-top: 12pt; margin-bottom: 6pt; border-bottom: Black 1.5pt solid"><p style="font: normal 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: center; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt"><!-- Field: Sequence; Type: Arabic; Name: PageNo -->12<!-- Field: /Sequence --></p></div><div style="break-before: page; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 12pt"><p style="text-align: center; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt"></p></div><!-- Field: /Page --><p style="text-align: center; font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: center"><span style="font-weight: bold;">CROWN RESERVE ACQUISITION CORP.  <ix:nonNumeric name="crac:PublicRightsTextBlock" contextRef="cref_111832914" escape="true" id="ixv-1700"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Note 6 &#8212; Public Rights</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> The Company issued <ix:nonFraction name="crac:UnitsIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues" contextRef="cref_1996041497" unitRef="uref_1275643730" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3532">17,250,000</ix:nonFraction> Public Rights as part of the IPO Units. Each Public Right automatically entitles the holder to receive one-fifth (1/5) of one Class A ordinary share upon the consummation of a Business Combination, for no additional consideration. No fractional shares will be issued upon exchange of the Public Rights; fractional entitlements will be rounded down. If the Company does not complete a Business Combination within the Combination Period, the Public Rights will expire worthless. </p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> The Public Rights are classified as permanent equity under ASC 815-40 because they are indexed to the Company&#8217;s own stock and do not contain any net-cash settlement provisions. Accordingly, <ix:nonFraction name="crac:UnitsOutstandingDuringPeriodShareIssues" contextRef="cref_111832914" unitRef="uref_1275643730" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3533">17,250,000</ix:nonFraction> outstanding Public Rights would result in the issuance of <ix:nonFraction name="crac:UnitsIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues" contextRef="cref_73196019" unitRef="uref_1275643730" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3534">3,450,000</ix:nonFraction> additional Class A ordinary shares upon consummation of a Business Combination. The Public Rights are not remeasured after initial recognition. </p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> The Company measured the fair value of the Public Rights at the IPO date using a probability-weighted expected return method (PWERM) based on a de-SPAC probability estimate of <ix:nonFraction name="crac:ConversionRatioPercentage" contextRef="cref_111832914" unitRef="uref_231006451" scale="-2" decimals="3" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3535">25.0</ix:nonFraction>%, a conversion ratio of 0.20, and a risk-free rate of <ix:nonFraction name="crac:PublicRightsMeasurementInput" contextRef="cref_111500216" unitRef="uref_231006451" scale="0" decimals="2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3536">3.52</ix:nonFraction>% over an estimated remaining term of <ix:nonFraction name="crac:PublicRightsMeasurementInput" contextRef="cref_1558101132" unitRef="uref_231006451" scale="0" decimals="2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3537">1.25</ix:nonFraction> years, resulting in a fair value of $<ix:nonFraction name="crac:AdjustmentsToAdditionalPaidInFairValue" contextRef="cref_1996041497" unitRef="uref_2008090408" scale="0" decimals="4" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3538">0.4778</ix:nonFraction> per right. The aggregate fair value of $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AdjustmentsToAdditionalPaidInCapitalMarkToMarket" contextRef="cref_111832914" unitRef="uref_1130685022" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3539">8,242,050</ix:nonFraction> (<ix:nonFraction name="crac:UnitsIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues" contextRef="cref_1647929904" unitRef="uref_1275643730" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3540">17,250,000</ix:nonFraction> &#215; $<ix:nonFraction name="crac:AdjustmentsToAdditionalPaidInFairValue" contextRef="cref_749116116" unitRef="uref_2008090408" scale="0" decimals="4" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3541">0.4778</ix:nonFraction>) was recorded as an increase to additional paid-in capital at the IPO date. </p></ix:nonNumeric> <p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&#160;</p><!-- Field: Page; Sequence: 15; Value: 1 --><div style="margin-top: 12pt; margin-bottom: 6pt; border-bottom: Black 1.5pt solid"><p style="font: normal 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: center; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt"><!-- Field: Sequence; Type: Arabic; Name: PageNo -->13<!-- Field: /Sequence --></p></div><div style="break-before: page; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 12pt"><p style="text-align: center; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt"></p></div><!-- Field: /Page --><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: center"><span style="font-weight: bold;">CROWN RESERVE ACQUISITION CORP.  <ix:nonNumeric name="crac:ClassAOrdinarySharesSubjectToPossibleRedemptionTextBlock" contextRef="cref_111832914" escape="true" id="ixv-1723"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Note 7 &#8212; Class A Ordinary Shares Subject to Possible Redemption</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> All <ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:TemporaryEquitySharesOutstanding" contextRef="cref_61118182" unitRef="uref_1275643730" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3542">17,250,000</ix:nonFraction> Class A ordinary shares sold as part of the Units in the IPO are classified as temporary equity (&#8220;Class A ordinary shares subject to possible redemption&#8221;) in accordance with ASC 480-10-S99-3A.  <ix:nonNumeric name="us-gaap:CommitmentsAndContingenciesDisclosureTextBlock" contextRef="cref_111832914" escape="true" id="ixv-1776"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Note 8 &#8212; Commitments and Contingencies</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;"><i>Registration and Shareholder Rights</i></span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The holders of the Founder Shares, Private Placement Units, and any shares that may be issued upon conversion of Working Capital Loans (and all underlying securities) are entitled to registration rights pursuant to a Registration and Shareholder Rights Agreement dated November 7, 2025, requiring the Company to register such securities for resale. The holders of these securities will be entitled to make up to three demands, excluding short form registration demands, that the Company register such securities. In addition, the holders have certain &#8220;piggy-back&#8221; registration rights with respect to registration statements filed subsequent to completion of a Business Combination and rights to require the Company to register for resale such securities pursuant to Rule 415 under the Securities Act. However, the Registration and Shareholder Rights Agreement provides that the Company will not be required to effect or permit any registration or cause any registration statement to become effective until the securities covered thereby are released from their lock-up restrictions. The Company will bear the expenses incurred in connection with the filing of any such registration statements.</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;"><i>Underwriting Agreement</i></span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> The underwriters were entitled to an upfront underwriting commission of <ix:nonFraction name="crac:UnderwritingDiscountPercentage" contextRef="cref_1351404180" unitRef="uref_231006451" scale="-2" decimals="3" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3546">1.0</ix:nonFraction>% of the gross IPO proceeds ($<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ProceedsFromIssuanceInitialPublicOffering" contextRef="cref_1505881784" unitRef="uref_1130685022" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3547">1,725,000</ix:nonFraction>) and will be entitled to a deferred underwriting commission of $<ix:nonFraction name="crac:DeferredUnderwritingCommissions" contextRef="cref_1351404180" unitRef="uref_1130685022" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3548">300,000</ix:nonFraction> upon completion of a Business Combination. The deferred underwriting commission will be paid from the Trust Account upon completion of a Business Combination; if there is no Business Combination, the deferred commission is forfeited. </p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;"><i>Business Combination Agreement</i></span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">As described in Note 1, on March 30, 2026, the Company entered into the Business Combination Agreement with Merger Sub and Carvix. Consummation of the transactions contemplated by the Business Combination Agreement is subject to customary conditions, including approval by the Company&#8217;s stockholders, the SEC declaring effective the related Registration Statement on Form S-4, and Nasdaq listing of the post-transaction company&#8217;s common stock.  <ix:nonNumeric name="us-gaap:EarningsPerShareTextBlock" contextRef="cref_111832914" escape="true" id="ixv-1860"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Note 10 &#8212; Net Loss Per Ordinary Share &#8212; Two-Class Method</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Company complies with the accounting and disclosure requirements of ASC 260, &#8220;Earnings Per Share.&#8221; Net income (or net loss) per ordinary share is computed by dividing net income (or net loss) by the weighted-average number of ordinary shares outstanding during the period. The Company applies the two-class method in calculating net income (or net loss) per ordinary share, as both Class A and Class B ordinary shares participate equally in dividends and other distributions. The accretion of Class A ordinary shares to redemption value is excluded from earnings (loss) per share as it is a direct equity transaction.</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; in addition, the Public Warrants were out-of-the-money at the reporting date (exercise price of $<ix:nonNumeric name="crac:WarrantsExpireTerm" contextRef="cref_1927099639" format="ixt-sec:duryear" id="ixv-3581">11.50</ix:nonNumeric> per share versus the underlying share price of $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SharesIssuedPricePerShare" contextRef="cref_2008090408" unitRef="uref_2008090408" scale="0" decimals="2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-3582">10.00</ix:nonFraction>) and would not have been dilutive on an in-the-money basis. Accordingly, diluted net loss per share equals basic net loss per share for both classes of ordinary shares. </p></ix:nonNumeric> <p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&#160;</p><!-- Field: Page; Sequence: 19; Value: 1 --><div style="margin-top: 12pt; margin-bottom: 6pt; border-bottom: Black 1.5pt solid"><p style="font: normal 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: center; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt"><!-- Field: Sequence; Type: Arabic; Name: PageNo -->17<!-- Field: /Sequence --></p></div><div style="break-before: page; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 12pt"><p style="text-align: center; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt"></p></div><!-- Field: /Page --><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: center"><span style="font-weight: bold;">CROWN RESERVE ACQUISITION CORP. I</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: center"><span style="font-weight: bold;">NOTES TO CONDENSED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: center"><span style="font-weight: bold;">MARCH 31, 2026</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: center">(Unaudited)</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: center">&#160;</p>
 <ix:nonNumeric name="us-gaap:SegmentReportingDisclosureTextBlock" contextRef="cref_111832914" escape="true" id="ixv-1999"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Note 11 &#8212; Segment Reporting</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Company complies with ASC 280, &#8220;Segment Reporting,&#8221; which establishes standards for reporting information about operating segments. Operating segments are defined as components of an enterprise for which separate financial information is available that is regularly evaluated by the Company&#8217;s chief operating decision maker (&#8220;CODM&#8221;) in deciding how to allocate resources and assess performance.</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> <ix:nonNumeric name="us-gaap:SegmentReportingExpenseInformationUsedByCodmDescription" contextRef="cref_111832914" id="ixv-2006">The Company&#8217;s CODM has been identified as the <span style="-sec-ix-hidden:fc_701645468">Chief Financial Officer</span>, who reviews operating results for the Company as a whole to make decisions about allocating resources and assessing financial performance. Accordingly, management has determined that the Company operates as a <span style="-sec-ix-hidden:fc_435621832">single</span> operating segment.</ix:nonNumeric> </p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The CODM assesses performance for the single segment and decides how to allocate resources based on net income (or net loss) as reported on the statement of operations. The measure of segment assets is reported on the balance sheet as total assets. Net income (or net loss) is reviewed and monitored by the CODM to manage and forecast cash to ensure adequate capital is available to complete a Business Combination or similar transaction within the Combination Period. The significant segment expenses provided to the CODM on a regular basis are general and administrative costs and professional fees; other components included in net income (or net loss) are reported on the statement of operations and described within their respective disclosures.</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><ix:nonNumeric name="us-gaap:ScheduleOfSegmentReportingInformationBySegmentTextBlock" contextRef="cref_111832914" escape="true" id="ixv-2012"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif;  <ix:nonNumeric name="us-gaap:SubsequentEventsTextBlock" contextRef="cref_111832914" escape="true" id="ixv-2045"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Note 12 &#8212; Subsequent Events</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">In accordance with ASC 855, &#8220;Subsequent Events,&#8221; the Company has evaluated subsequent events and transactions that occurred after the balance sheet date through the date these condensed financial statements were available to be issued. Based on this review, &#160; the Company did not identify any subsequent events that would have required adjustment or disclosure in the condensed financial statements.</p><ix:exclude><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> </p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p></ix:exclude></ix:nonNumeric> <!-- Field: Page; Sequence: 20; Value: 1 --><div style="margin-top: 12pt; margin-bottom: 6pt; border-bottom: Black 1.5pt solid"><p style="font: normal 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: center; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt"><!-- Field: Sequence; Type: Arabic; Name: PageNo -->18<!-- Field: /Sequence --></p></div><div style="break-before: page; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 12pt"><p style="text-align: center; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt"></p></div><!-- Field: /Page --><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="font-weight: bold;"><span id="a_008" style="display: inline-block;"></span>Item 2. Management&#8217;s Discussion and Analysis of Financial
Condition and Results of Operations.</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">References in this Quarterly Report on Form 10-Q
(this &#8220;Quarterly Report&#8221;) to &#8220;we,&#8221; &#8220;us,&#8221; &#8220;our&#8221; or the &#8220;Company&#8221; refer to Crown
Reserve Acquisition Corp. I. References to our &#8220;management&#8221; or our &#8220;management team&#8221; refer to our officers and
directors, and references to the &#8220;Sponsor&#8221; refer to Crown Acquisition Sponsor LLC. The following discussion and analysis of
the Company&#8217;s financial condition and results of operations should be read in conjunction with our condensed financial statements
and the related notes thereto contained elsewhere in this Quarterly Report and our audited financial statements and the related notes
thereto for the period from April 29, 2025 (inception) through December 31, 2025 included in our Annual Report on Form 10-K filed with
the SEC. Certain information contained in the discussion and analysis set forth below includes forward-looking statements that involve
risks and uncertainties.</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Special Note Regarding Forward-Looking Statements</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">This Quarterly Report includes &#8220;forward-looking
statements&#8221; within the meaning of Section 27A of the Securities Act and Section 21E of the Exchange Act that are not historical

&#8220;Management&#8217;s Discussion and Analysis of Financial Condition and Results of Operations&#8221; regarding the Company&#8217;s
financial position, business strategy, the Business Combination Agreement with Carvix, and the plans and objectives of management for
